    const content = 'Valid for admissions taking place in September 2026 cycle.';
    const meta = detectTemporalMetadata(content, 'September 2026');
    assert.strictEqual(meta.temporalStatus, 'current');
  });

  runTest('detectTemporalMetadata flags undated content', () => {
    const content = 'Hostel allowances are granted to all students residing in approved residences.';
    const meta = detectTemporalMetadata(content, '');
    assert.strictEqual(meta.temporalStatus, 'undated');
  });

  // -------------------------------------------------------------

  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  console.log('\n--- GROUP 6: Service Integration & Offline Resilience ---');

  await runAsyncTest('analyzeSourcesWithAI seamlessly returns precomputed analysis when offline', async () => {
    const res = await analyzeSourcesWithAI({
      topic: 'What should I verify before applying for the State Post-Matric Scholarship?',
      category: 'Education',
      sources: sampleScenarios[0].sources,
      forceDemoMode: true,
    });

    assert.strictEqual(res.success, true);
    assert.strictEqual(res.meta.isOfflineDemo, true);
    assert.strictEqual(res.analysis.readinessScore, 54);
    assert(res.analysis.contradictions.length >= 2);
    assert(res.analysis.informationGaps.length >= 2);
    assert(res.analysis.rubricStatus.length === 6);
  });

  await runAsyncTest('analyzeSourcesWithAI handles unknown custom topic with dynamic fallback', async () => {
    const res = await analyzeSourcesWithAI({
      topic: 'How to register a drone in Mumbai under DGCA rules?',
      category: 'Legal',
      sources: [
        { id: 'src-1', title: 'DGCA Circular', content: 'Nano drones under 250 grams do not require UIN registration.' },
        { id: 'src-2', title: 'Drone Pilot Blog', content: 'All drones including nano require mandatory digital sky permission.' },
      ],
      forceDemoMode: true,
    });

    assert.strictEqual(res.success, true);
    assert(res.analysis.readinessScore > 0);
    assert(res.analysis.contradictions.length >= 1);
    assert(res.analysis.informationGaps.length >= 1);
  });

  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  // TEST GROUP 7: DYNAMIC .ENV MODEL FETCHING & REGISTRATION
  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  console.log('\n--- GROUP 7: Dynamic .env Model Fetching & Registration ---');

  const {
    getModelsFromEnv,
    getAvailableModels,
    getDefaultModel,
    sanitizeModelSlug,
  } = require('../config/openRouterConfig');

  runTest('openRouterConfig fetches models strictly from process.env.GEMINI_MODEL or process.env.OPENROUTER_MODEL', () => {
    const rawEnv = process.env.GEMINI_MODEL || process.env.OPENROUTER_MODEL || '';
    const expectedIds = rawEnv.split(',').map((s) => sanitizeModelSlug(s)).filter(Boolean);
    const parsedModels = getModelsFromEnv();

    assert(parsedModels.length > 0, 'Parsed models list must not be empty');
    if (expectedIds.length > 0) {
      assert.strictEqual(parsedModels.length, expectedIds.length);
      assert.strictEqual(parsedModels[0], expectedIds[0]);
    }
  });

  runTest('getAvailableModels generates metadata matching .env models', () => {
    const models = getAvailableModels();
    const envModels = getModelsFromEnv();

    assert.strictEqual(models.length, envModels.length);
    assert.strictEqual(models[0].id, envModels[0]);
    assert(models[0].badge.includes('Primary'));
    assert(models[0].name.length > 0);
    assert(models[0].provider.length > 0);
  });

  runTest('getDefaultModel strictly returns first model configured in .env', () => {
    const defaultModel = getDefaultModel();
    const envModels = getModelsFromEnv();
    assert.strictEqual(defaultModel, envModels[0]);
  });

  runTest('sanitizeModelSlug cleanly strips prefixes (google/, models/) and :free suffix', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(
      sanitizeModelSlug('google/gemini-2.5-flash'),
      'gemini-2.5-flash'
    );
    assert.strictEqual(
      sanitizeModelSlug('models/gemini-2.0-flash'),
      'gemini-2.0-flash'
    );
    assert.strictEqual(
      sanitizeModelSlug('gemini-2.5-flash-lite:free'),
      'gemini-2.5-flash-lite'
    );
    assert.strictEqual(
      sanitizeModelSlug('gemini-2.5-flash'),
      'gemini-2.5-flash'
    );
  });

  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  // TEST GROUP 8: ROBUST JSON SANITIZATION & LLM RESPONSE ENGINE
  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  console.log('\n--- GROUP 8: Robust JSON Sanitization & Parsing Engine ---');

  runTest('cleanAndParseJSON strips reasoning <think> blocks and markdown fences', () => {
    const raw = `<think>
Evaluating contradictions and missing income ceilings...
Candidate parameters: Rs 2.5L vs Rs 8.0L
</think>
\`\`\`json
{
  "readinessScore": 60,
  "readinessLabel": "Moderate Risk",
  "expectedRubric": ["Income Ceiling", "Renewal Rules"]
}
\`\`\`
Audit complete.`;

    const parsed = cleanAndParseJSON(raw);
    assert.strictEqual(parsed.readinessScore, 60);
    assert.strictEqual(parsed.readinessLabel, 'Moderate Risk');
    assert.strictEqual(parsed.expectedRubric.length, 2);
  });

  runTest('cleanAndParseJSON automatically cleans trailing commas before closing braces/brackets', () => {
    const rawWithTrailingCommas = `{
      "readinessScore": 45,
      "expectedRubric": [
        "Aadhaar Proof",
        "Tehsildar Certificate",
      ],
      "contradictions": [
        {
          "parameter": "Income Limit",
          "severity": "High",
        },
      ],
    }`;

    const parsed = cleanAndParseJSON(rawWithTrailingCommas);
    assert.strictEqual(parsed.readinessScore, 45);
    assert.strictEqual(parsed.expectedRubric.length, 2);
    assert.strictEqual(parsed.contradictions.length, 1);
    assert.strictEqual(parsed.contradictions[0].parameter, 'Income Limit');
  });

  runTest('cleanAndParseJSON recovers from unescaped raw newlines & tabs inside evidenceQuotes', () => {
    const rawWithUnescapedControlChars = `{
      "readinessScore": 54,
      "sourcedFacts": [
        {
          "id": "fact-1",
          "statement": "State domicile is mandatory.",
          "evidenceQuote": "Government of Maharashtra Circular No. 44\nDate: 12 Jan 2026\tMandatory Domicile Required"
        }
      ]
    }`;

    const parsed = cleanAndParseJSON(rawWithUnescapedControlChars);
    assert.strictEqual(parsed.readinessScore, 54);
    assert.strictEqual(parsed.sourcedFacts.length, 1);
    assert(parsed.sourcedFacts[0].evidenceQuote.includes('Circular No. 44'));
  });

  runTest('cleanAndParseJSON safely throws on non-JSON content without process crash', () => {
    let threw = false;
    try {
      cleanAndParseJSON('Sorry, as an AI model I cannot fulfill this request right now.');
    } catch (err) {
      threw = true;
      assert(err.message.includes('No valid JSON object structure found'));
    }
    assert.strictEqual(threw, true);
  });

  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  // SUMMARY
  // -------------------------------------------------------------
  console.log('\n================================================================');
  console.log(`  TEST RESULTS: ${passedTests} OF ${totalTests} TESTS PASSED`);
  if (passedTests === totalTests) {
    console.log('  STATUS: ALL TESTS PASSED - 100% SUCCESS');
  } else {
    console.log(`  STATUS: ${totalTests - passedTests} TESTS FAILED`);
  }
  console.log('================================================================\n');

  if (passedTests !== totalTests) {
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

executeAllTests();

FILE: frontend/src/components/ActionFooter.jsx

import React from 'react';
import {
  Sparkles,
  CheckCircle2,
  AlertTriangle,
  Clock,
  Cpu,
  Loader2,
  ArrowRight
} from 'lucide-react';

export default function ActionFooter({
  topic,
  sources,
  activeModel,
  hasOpenRouterKey,
  hasGeminiKey,
  forceOfflineDemo,
  isAnalyzing,
  onTriggerAnalysis,
  analysisNotice,
}) {
  const isTopicValid = Boolean(topic && topic.trim().length >= 8);
  const validSources = sources.filter(
    (s) => s.content && s.content.trim().length >= 10
  );
  const hasEnoughSources = validSources.length >= 2;
  const undatedSources = validSources.filter(
    (s) => !s.publishedDate || !s.publishedDate.trim()
  );
  const hasUndatedWarning = undatedSources.length > 0;

  const isFormValid = isTopicValid && hasEnoughSources;
  const hasKey = Boolean(hasGeminiKey || hasOpenRouterKey);

  // Friendly short model label from .env
  const getShortModel = (model) => {
    if (!model) return 'Configured Model (.env)';
    const parts = model.split('/');
    const rawName = (parts.length > 1 ? parts.slice(1).join('/') : parts[0]).replace(/:free$/i, '');
    const cleanName = rawName
      .split(/[-_]/)
      .map((word) => word.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + word.slice(1))
      .join(' ');
    return `${cleanName}${model.includes(':free') ? ' (Free)' : ''}`;
  };

  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm backdrop-blur-md transition-colors duration-200 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900/95 sm:p-6">
      
      {/* Top Bar: Validation Badges, Undated Warning & Active Model Badge */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 pb-4">
        
        {/* Validation Badges */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          
          {/* Topic check */}
          <span
            className={`inline-flex items-center space-x-1.5 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium border ${
              isTopicValid
                ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-300 dark:bg-emerald-950/50 dark:text-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-800/60'
                : 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-800 border-rose-300 dark:bg-rose-950/50 dark:text-rose-300 dark:border-rose-800/60'
            }`}
          >
            {isTopicValid ? (
              <CheckCircle2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400" />
            ) : (
              <AlertTriangle className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400" />
            )}
            <span>{isTopicValid ? 'Topic Specified' : 'Research Goal Missing'}</span>
          </span>

          {/* Sources count check */}
          <span
            className={`inline-flex items-center space-x-1.5 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium border ${
              hasEnoughSources
                ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-300 dark:bg-emerald-950/50 dark:text-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-800/60'
                : 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 border-amber-300 dark:bg-amber-950/50 dark:text-amber-300 dark:border-amber-800/60'
            }`}
          >
            {hasEnoughSources ? (
              <CheckCircle2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400" />
            ) : (
              <AlertTriangle className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400" />
            )}
            <span>
              {validSources.length} of 2 required sources loaded
            </span>
          </span>

          {/* Undated Temporal warning badge */}
          {hasUndatedWarning && (
            <span className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1.5 rounded-lg border border-amber-300 bg-amber-50 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-amber-800 dark:border-amber-800/60 dark:bg-amber-950/40 dark:text-amber-300">
              <Clock className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 shrink-0" />
              <span>{undatedSources.length} Undated source â€” will trigger Temporal Uncertainty check</span>
            </span>
          )}

        </div>

        {/* Selected AI Model Badge */}
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
          <Cpu className="h-4 w-4 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400" />
          <span>Engine:</span>
          <span className="font-mono text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300 font-semibold bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-950 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800">
            {getShortModel(activeModel)}
          </span>
          <span
            className={`rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold border ${
              forceOfflineDemo
                ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border-amber-300 dark:bg-amber-950 dark:text-amber-300 dark:border-amber-800'
                : hasKey
                ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-300 dark:bg-emerald-950 dark:text-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-800'
                : 'bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-800 border-indigo-200 dark:bg-indigo-950 dark:text-indigo-300 dark:border-indigo-800'
            }`}
          >
            {forceOfflineDemo ? 'Demo Mode' : hasKey ? 'Live AI' : 'Preset'}
          </span>
        </div>

      </div>

      {/* Notice / Feedback Banner if analysis was clicked */}
      {analysisNotice && (
        <div
          className={`mt-4 rounded-xl p-3 text-xs border ${
            analysisNotice.type === 'success'
              ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-300 dark:bg-emerald-950/50 dark:text-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-800'
              : 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-800 border-rose-300 dark:bg-rose-950/50 dark:text-rose-300 dark:border-rose-800'
          }`}
        >
          <div className="flex items-start space-x-2">
            {analysisNotice.type === 'success' ? (
              <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 mt-0.5" />
            ) : (
              <AlertTriangle className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 mt-0.5" />
            )}
            <div>
              <div className="font-semibold">{analysisNotice.title}</div>
              <div className="mt-0.5 text-[11px] opacity-90">{analysisNotice.message}</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Action Bar */}
      <div className="mt-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-4">
        
        <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
          <p className="font-medium text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-300">
            Multi-Source Workspace Validated & Ready
          </p>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
            Cross-examines documents, flags contradictions, and exposes digital dark spots.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Primary CTA Trigger Button */}
        <button
          type="button"
          disabled={!isFormValid || isAnalyzing}
          onClick={onTriggerAnalysis}
          className={`inline-flex items-center justify-center space-x-2.5 rounded-xl px-6 py-3.5 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-lg transition-all ${
            !isFormValid || isAnalyzing
              ? 'cursor-not-allowed bg-slate-300 text-slate-500 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-600'
              : 'bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 via-indigo-500 to-violet-600 hover:from-indigo-500 hover:to-violet-500 shadow-indigo-600/25 hover:shadow-indigo-500/40 hover:scale-[1.01] active:scale-[0.99]'
          }`}
        >
          {isAnalyzing ? (
            <>
              <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin text-white" />
              <span>Cross-Examining Sources...</span>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <Sparkles className="h-4 w-4 text-indigo-200" />
              <span>Analyze Sources for Gaps & Contradictions</span>
              <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4 text-indigo-200" />
            </>
          )}
        </button>

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

FILE: frontend/src/components/AnalysisLoadingOverlay.jsx
================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  Sparkles,
  Search,
  Calendar,
  GitCompare,
  ListChecks,
  Loader2,
} from 'lucide-react';

const ANALYSIS_STEPS = [
  {
    icon: Search,
    label: 'Ingesting and normalizing text across sources...',
  },
  {
    icon: Calendar,
    label: 'Evaluating 2026 temporal currency and cycle dates...',
  },
  {
    icon: GitCompare,
    label: 'Cross-examining statements for direct contradictions...',
  },
  {
    icon: Sparkles,
    label: 'Auditing expected decision rubric for dark spots...',
  },
  {
    icon: ListChecks,
    label: 'Synthesizing prioritized official verification checklist...',
  },
];

export default function AnalysisLoadingOverlay({ activeModel, isVisible = true }) {
  const [currentStepIndex, setCurrentStepIndex] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isVisible) {
      setCurrentStepIndex(0);
      return;
    }

    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      setCurrentStepIndex((prev) => (prev < ANALYSIS_STEPS.length - 1 ? prev + 1 : prev));
    }, 1500);

    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [isVisible]);

  if (!isVisible) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-xs animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div className="relative w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-white p-6 shadow-2xl transition-colors dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 text-center space-y-5">
        
        {/* Minimal Icon */}
        <div className="relative mx-auto flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-900 shadow-soft">
          <Sparkles className="h-6 w-6 animate-spin" style={{ animationDuration: '6s' }} />
          <span className="absolute -bottom-1 -right-1 flex h-4 w-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-zinc-700 text-[9px] text-white">
            <Loader2 className="h-2.5 w-2.5 animate-spin" />
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* Headings */}
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white">
            Cross-Auditing Sources
          </h3>
          <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400">
            Evaluating regulatory corpus using{' '}
            <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white">
              {activeModel?.split('/')[1]?.replace(':free', '') || 'AI Audit Engine'}
            </span>
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Dynamic Progress Steps */}
        <div className="space-y-2 text-left">
          {ANALYSIS_STEPS.map((s, idx) => {
            const Icon = s.icon;
            const isDone = idx < currentStepIndex;
            const isCurrent = idx === currentStepIndex;

            return (
              <div
                key={idx}
                className={`flex items-center space-x-2.5 rounded-xl p-2.5 text-xs transition-all ${
                  isCurrent
                    ? 'border border-zinc-900/40 bg-zinc-100 font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-white'
                    : isDone
                    ? 'text-zinc-400 opacity-60'
                    : 'text-zinc-300 dark:text-zinc-700'
                }`}
              >
                <div
                  className={`flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md ${
                    isCurrent
                      ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-900'
                      : isDone
                      ? 'bg-zinc-200 text-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-300'
                      : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-400 dark:bg-zinc-850 dark:text-zinc-600'
                  }`}
                >
                  <Icon className="h-3 w-3" />
                </div>
                <span className="truncate">{s.label}</span>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        <p className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 font-mono">
          Detecting omissions &bull; Cross-checking limits &bull; Flagging drift
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: frontend/src/components/ApiKeyModal.jsx

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  X,
  KeyRound,
  Check,
  AlertTriangle,
  Loader2,
  Trash2,
  Eye,
  EyeOff,
  Cpu,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { probeGemini } from '../services/api';

export default function ApiKeyModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  currentKey,
  currentModel,
  availableModels = [],
  onSaveConfig,
  backendHasKey,
  forceOfflineDemo,
  onToggleForceDemo,
}) {
  const [apiKey, setApiKey] = useState(currentKey || '');
  const [selectedModel, setSelectedModel] = useState(
    currentModel || availableModels[0]?.id || ''
  );
  const [showKey, setShowKey] = useState(false);
  const [testing, setTesting] = useState(false);
  const [testResult, setTestResult] = useState(null);
  const [saveToast, setSaveToast] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const key = currentKey || '';
    const initialModel = currentModel || availableModels[0]?.id || '';
    setApiKey(key);
    setSelectedModel(initialModel);
    setTestResult(null);
  }, [isOpen, currentKey, currentModel, availableModels]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape' && isOpen) {
        onClose();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [isOpen, onClose]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    } else {
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
    }
    return () => {
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
    };
  }, [isOpen]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleTestKey = async () => {
    const keyToTest = apiKey.trim() || currentKey;
    const modelToTest = selectedModel || availableModels[0]?.id || 'gemini-2.5-flash';

    setTesting(true);
    setTestResult(null);

    const result = await probeGemini(keyToTest, modelToTest);
    setTesting(false);
    setTestResult(result);
  };

  const handleSave = () => {
    const finalModel = selectedModel || availableModels[0]?.id || '';
    onSaveConfig(apiKey.trim(), finalModel);
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      localStorage.setItem('gaplens_gemini_key', apiKey.trim());
      localStorage.setItem('gaplens_gemini_model', finalModel);
    }
    setSaveToast(true);
    setTimeout(() => {
      setSaveToast(false);
      onClose();
    }, 1200);
  };

  const handleClearKey = () => {
    setApiKey('');
    const defaultMod = availableModels[0]?.id || '';
    onSaveConfig('', defaultMod);
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      localStorage.removeItem('gaplens_gemini_key');
      localStorage.removeItem('gaplens_openrouter_key');
    }
    setTestResult({
      success: true,
      message: 'Client API key cleared. GapLens will use backend key or verified offline fallback.',
    });
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 overflow-y-auto no-print">
      <div
        className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-xs transition-opacity"
        onClick={onClose}
      />

      <div className="relative w-full max-w-xl rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-white p-6 shadow-2xl transition-all dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 z-10 space-y-5">
        
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-zinc-100 pb-3 dark:border-zinc-800">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2.5">
            <span className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-900">
              <Cpu className="h-4 w-4" />
            </span>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white">
                AI Model &amp; API Configuration
              </h3>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400">
                Configure Google Gemini models from backend .env or provide custom API key
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="rounded-full p-1.5 text-zinc-400 hover:bg-zinc-100 hover:text-zinc-700 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:hover:text-zinc-200"
          >
            <X className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Offline Demo Mode Toggle */}
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-zinc-50 p-3.5 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-850 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="space-y-0.5">
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white block">
              Offline Demo Mode
            </span>
            <span className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500">
              Bypasses live LLM calls and serves deterministic audit datasets.
            </span>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onToggleForceDemo && onToggleForceDemo(!forceOfflineDemo)}
            className={`relative inline-flex h-6 w-11 shrink-0 cursor-pointer rounded-full border-2 border-transparent transition-colors duration-200 ease-in-out ${
              forceOfflineDemo ? 'bg-zinc-900 dark:bg-zinc-100' : 'bg-zinc-300 dark:bg-zinc-700'
            }`}
          >
            <span
              className={`pointer-events-none inline-block h-5 w-5 transform rounded-full bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 shadow-sm transition duration-200 ease-in-out ${
                forceOfflineDemo ? 'translate-x-5' : 'translate-x-0'
              }`}
            />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* API Key Input */}
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-500">
            Google Gemini API Key (Optional)
          </label>
          <div className="relative">
            <input
              type={showKey ? 'text' : 'password'}
              value={apiKey}
              onChange={(e) => setApiKey(e.target.value)}
              placeholder={backendHasKey ? 'Server Gemini key configured via .env' : 'AIzaSy...'}
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-zinc-50 py-2.5 pl-3 pr-20 text-xs font-mono text-zinc-900 focus:border-zinc-900 focus:bg-white focus:outline-hidden dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-850 dark:text-zinc-100"
            />
            <div className="absolute inset-y-0 right-2 flex items-center space-x-1">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowKey(!showKey)}
                className="p-1 text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-700"
              >
                {showKey ? <EyeOff className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> : <Eye className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />}
              </button>
              {apiKey && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleClearKey}
                  className="p-1 text-zinc-400 hover:text-rose-600"
                  title="Clear key"
                >
                  <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Model Selection Strictly from .env */}
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-500">
            Select Active Model (.env)
          </label>
          {availableModels.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-zinc-300 dark:border-zinc-700 p-4 text-center text-xs text-zinc-500">
              No models found in backend .env (configure GEMINI_MODEL in backend/.env)
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 max-h-48 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
              {availableModels.map((m) => {
                const isSelected = selectedModel === m.id;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={m.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setSelectedModel(m.id)}
                    className={`rounded-xl border p-3 text-left transition-all ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'border-zinc-900 bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:border-white dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-900 shadow-sm'
                        : 'border-zinc-200/80 bg-zinc-50 text-zinc-700 hover:bg-white dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-850 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <span className="text-xs font-bold truncate">{m.name}</span>
                      <span
                        className={`rounded-full px-2 py-0.2 text-[9px] font-semibold ${
                          isSelected
                            ? 'bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-200 dark:text-zinc-800'
                            : 'bg-zinc-200 text-zinc-600 dark:bg-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {m.badge || 'Configured'}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <p
                      className={`mt-1 text-[11px] line-clamp-1 ${
                        isSelected ? 'text-zinc-300 dark:text-zinc-600' : 'text-zinc-400'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {m.description}
                    </p>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Test Result Message */}
        {testResult && (
          <div
            className={`rounded-xl p-2.5 text-xs flex items-center space-x-2 border ${
              testResult.success
                ? 'bg-zinc-50 text-emerald-600 border-zinc-200/60 dark:bg-zinc-850 dark:border-zinc-800'
                : 'bg-zinc-50 text-rose-600 border-zinc-200/60 dark:bg-zinc-850 dark:border-zinc-800'
            }`}
          >
            {testResult.success ? <Check className="h-4 w-4" /> : <AlertTriangle className="h-4 w-4" />}
            <span>{testResult.message}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Footer Actions */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-zinc-100 pt-3 dark:border-zinc-800">
          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={testing}
            onClick={handleTestKey}
            className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1.5 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-850 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors"
          >
            {testing ? <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" /> : <KeyRound className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />}
            <span>Test Connection</span>
          </button>

          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-900"
            >
              Cancel
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleSave}
              className="rounded-xl bg-zinc-900 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-900 dark:hover:bg-zinc-200 shadow-soft"
            >
              {saveToast ? 'Saved!' : 'Save & Apply'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

                  <div className="font-semibold text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-300 flex items-center justify-between">
                    <span>Renewal & Year-2 Progression Criteria</span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400">CRITICAL GAP</span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 text-[11px]">
                    None of the 3 sources mention minimum attendance percentage or CGPA required to retain the scholarship in subsequent academic years.
                  </p>
                </div>
                <div className="rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50/50 p-3 dark:border-amber-900/40 dark:bg-amber-950/20">
                  <div className="font-semibold text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-300 flex items-center justify-between">
                    <span>Portal Closing Cutoff Deadline</span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400">OMITTED FACT</span>
                  </div>

                    Source 1 mentions online submission but omits the final application closing date for the 2026-27 cycle.
                  </p>

            ) : (
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-slate-50/70 p-6 text-center dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-950/40">

            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">

                <GitCompare className="h-4 w-4" />
              </span>
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">Panel 3: Contradiction & Conflict Radar</h3>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-400 font-medium">Direct Cross-Source Conflicts</span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <span className="rounded bg-rose-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-mono text-rose-800 border border-rose-300 dark:bg-rose-950/80 dark:text-rose-300 dark:border-rose-800/60">
              Conflict Engine
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-4">
            {showSimulatedData ? (
              <div className="space-y-2 text-xs">
                <div className="rounded-xl border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50/50 p-3 dark:border-rose-900/40 dark:bg-rose-950/20">
                  <div className="font-semibold text-rose-800 dark:text-rose-300">
                    Conflict: Family Income Eligibility Ceiling
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-2 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-[11px]">
                    <div className="rounded bg-white p-2 border border-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-950 dark:border-slate-800">
                      <span className="font-mono text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-400 font-bold">[Source 1]:</span> Rs. 2,50,000 maximum annual income limit.
                    </div>
                    <div className="rounded bg-white p-2 border border-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-950 dark:border-slate-800">
                      <span className="font-mono text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-400 font-bold">[Source 2]:</span> Claims limit is up to Rs. 8,00,000.
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="rounded-xl border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50/50 p-3 dark:border-rose-900/40 dark:bg-rose-950/20">
                  <div className="font-semibold text-rose-800 dark:text-rose-300">
                    Conflict: Income Proof Format
                  </div>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 text-[11px]">
                    Tehsildar certificate required by Official Notice vs Self-attested Rs. 100 Stamp Paper stated in third-party blog.
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-slate-50/70 p-6 text-center dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-950/40">
                <GitCompare className="h-8 w-8 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-600 mb-2" />
                <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-300">Source Conflict Matrix</div>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-slate-500 max-w-xs">
                  Pinpoints conflicting numerical criteria, diverging eligibility thresholds, and incompatible instructions across inputs.
                </p>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Panel 4: Outdated & Undated Source Warnings */}
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm relative overflow-hidden transition-colors duration-200 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900/80">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 pb-3">
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
              <span className="flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-purple-100 text-purple-700 ring-1 ring-purple-300 dark:bg-purple-950 dark:text-purple-400 dark:ring-purple-800">
                <CalendarClock className="h-4 w-4" />
              </span>
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">Panel 4: Temporal & Outdated Warnings</h3>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-purple-700 dark:text-purple-400 font-medium">Stale Cycles & Temporal Drift</span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <span className="rounded bg-purple-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-mono text-purple-800 border border-purple-300 dark:bg-purple-950/80 dark:text-purple-300 dark:border-purple-800/60">
              Temporal Radar
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-4">
            {showSimulatedData ? (
              <div className="space-y-2 text-xs">
                <div className="rounded-xl border border-purple-200 bg-purple-50/50 p-3 dark:border-purple-900/40 dark:bg-purple-950/20">
                  <div className="font-semibold text-purple-800 dark:text-purple-300 flex items-center justify-between">
                    <span>Archived 2023-24 Circular Superseded</span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-purple-700 dark:text-purple-400">[Source 3]</span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 text-[11px]">
                    Source 3 mandates in-person submission to District Nodal Officer by Nov 2023. This is an outdated historical cycle superseded by 2026 digital portal workflow.
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-slate-50/70 p-6 text-center dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-950/40">
                <CalendarClock className="h-8 w-8 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-600 mb-2" />
                <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-300">Temporal Staleness Detector</div>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-slate-500 max-w-xs">
                  Flags obsolete application windows, legacy offline procedures, and unverified undated guides.
                </p>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

      </div>

      {/* Panel 5: Prioritized Verification Checklist (Spans full width) */}
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm relative overflow-hidden transition-colors duration-200 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900/80">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 pb-3">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <span className="flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 ring-1 ring-indigo-300 dark:bg-indigo-950 dark:text-indigo-400 dark:ring-indigo-800">
              <ListChecks className="h-4 w-4" />
            </span>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">Panel 5: Prioritized Next-Step Verification Checklist</h3>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-400 font-medium">Actionable Resolution Path</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <span className="rounded bg-indigo-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-mono text-indigo-800 border border-indigo-200 dark:bg-indigo-950/80 dark:text-indigo-300 dark:border-indigo-800/60">
            Action Items
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-4">
          {showSimulatedData ? (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2.5 sm:grid-cols-3 text-xs">
              <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-3.5 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-950">
                <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-rose-700 dark:text-rose-400 font-semibold mb-1">
                  <span className="rounded bg-rose-100 dark:bg-rose-950 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] border border-rose-300 dark:border-rose-800">Priority 1</span>
                  <span>Confirm Income Limit</span>
                </div>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                  Verify with college nodal desk whether Rs. 2.5L is for general quota or if Rs. 8L applies to specific category exemptions.
                </p>
              </div>
              <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-3.5 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-950">
                <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400 font-semibold mb-1">
                  <span className="rounded bg-amber-100 dark:bg-amber-950 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] border border-amber-300 dark:border-amber-800">Priority 2</span>
                  <span>Official Tehsildar Certificate</span>
                </div>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                  Do not rely on the stamp paper affidavit mentioned in third-party guides; secure the digital Tehsildar certificate.
                </p>
              </div>
              <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-3.5 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-950">
                <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-400 font-semibold mb-1">
                  <span className="rounded bg-indigo-100 dark:bg-indigo-950 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] border border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-800">Priority 3</span>
                  <span>Ignore Offline 2023 Submission</span>
                </div>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                  Submit exclusively via the 2026-27 state portal; physical nodal officer submission is deprecated.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-slate-50/70 p-6 text-center dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-950/40">
              <ListChecks className="h-8 w-8 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-600 mb-2" />
              <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-300">Actionable Resolution Checklist</div>
              <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-slate-500 max-w-md">
                Translates detected information gaps and cross-source conflicts into an ordered checklist for the user to resolve before submitting applications or signing contracts.
              </p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

FILE: frontend/src/components/InquiryDraftModal.jsx
================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  X,
  Copy,
  Check,
  Mail,
  Building2,
  FileText,
  ExternalLink,
} from 'lucide-react';

export default function InquiryDraftModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  topic = '',
  item = null,
  allChecklist = [],
  informationGaps = [],
  contradictions = [],
}) {
  const [inquiryType, setInquiryType] = useState('email'); // 'email' | 'in_person'
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape' && isOpen) {
        onClose();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [isOpen, onClose]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    } else {
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
    }
    return () => {
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
    };
  }, [isOpen]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const targetAuthority = item?.targetAuthority || 'Competent Nodal Authority';
  const actionItem = item?.actionItem || 'Verification of eligibility and mandatory filing deadlines';

  const emailSubject = `Inquiry / Official Clarification Request: ${topic.slice(0, 70)} (Ref: 2026 Guidelines)`;

  const emailBody = `To,
The Nodal Officer / Public Information Helpdesk,
${targetAuthority}

Subject: ${emailSubject}

Respected Officer / Scheme Administrator,

I am writing to respectfully request official clarification regarding the statutory guidelines, eligibility limits, and procedural requirements for: "${topic}".

During our preliminary cross-verification of published guidelines, critical discrepancies and unaddressed compliance parameters were identified that require your authoritative confirmation:

1. ACTION ITEM CURRENTLY UNDER VERIFICATION:
â€¢ ${actionItem}
â€¢ Reason Flagged: ${item?.reason || 'Clarification required prior to formal filing'}

${
  contradictions.length > 0
    ? `2. CONFLICTING STATUTORY CLAIMS REQUIRING CLARIFICATION:
${contradictions
  .map(
    (c, i) =>
      `â€¢ Parameter: ${c.parameter}
   - Claim A: "${c.sourceA?.claim}" (${c.sourceA?.title})
   - Claim B: "${c.sourceB?.claim}" (${c.sourceB?.title})`
  )
  .join('\n\n')}`
    : ''
}

${
  informationGaps.length > 0
    ? `3. UNADDRESSED POLICIES / MISSING INFORMATION:
${informationGaps
  .map(
    (g, i) =>
      `â€¢ Missing Requirement: ${g.title}
   - Specific Question: ${g.missingQuestion}`
  )
  .join('\n')}`
    : ''
}

Could you please confirm the legally binding 2026 regulations, official cutoff deadlines, and mandatory documentation required?

Thank you for your assistance.

Sincerely,
Concerned Citizen / Applicant
Contact: [Your Phone / Email]
Generated via GapLens Regulatory Verification Engine (Baseline Year: 2026)`;

  const inPersonBrief = `GAPLENS IN-PERSON AUTHORITY VERIFICATION DRAFT
============================================================
TOPIC: ${topic}
TARGET AUTHORITY: ${targetAuthority}
DATE: ${new Date().toLocaleDateString()}
============================================================

KEY QUESTION TO ASK AT HELP-DESK:
"${actionItem}"

CONTEXT / WHY THIS MATTERS:
${item?.reason || 'Clarification required to prevent statutory rejections'}

SPECIFIC POINTS TO VERIFY:
${contradictions
  .slice(0, 2)
  .map((c, i) => `[ ] Conflict on "${c.parameter}": Which threshold is currently binding?`)
  .join('\n')}
${informationGaps
  .slice(0, 2)
  .map((g, i) => `[ ] Missing Parameter: "${g.missingQuestion}"`)
  .join('\n')}

DESK NOTES / OFFICER NAME:
Officer Met: ________________________
Designation: ________________________
Confirmation Received: __________________________________________________
Official Circular Number Cited: _________________________________________`;

  const activeText = inquiryType === 'email' ? emailBody : inPersonBrief;

  const handleCopy = () => {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(activeText);
    setCopied(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
  };

  const handleOpenMailClient = () => {
    const mailto = `mailto:?subject=${encodeURIComponent(
      emailSubject
    )}&body=${encodeURIComponent(emailBody)}`;
    window.open(mailto, '_blank');
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 overflow-y-auto no-print">
      <div
        className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-xs transition-opacity"
        onClick={onClose}
      />

      <div className="relative w-full max-w-2xl rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-white p-6 shadow-2xl transition-all dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 z-10 space-y-4">
        
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-zinc-100 pb-3 dark:border-zinc-800">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2.5">
            <span className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-900">
              <Mail className="h-4 w-4" />
            </span>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white">
                Official Authority Inquiry Generator
              </h3>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400">
                Pre-composed formal inquiries targeting {targetAuthority}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="rounded-full p-1.5 text-zinc-400 hover:bg-zinc-100 hover:text-zinc-700 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:hover:text-zinc-200"
          >
            <X className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Format Selector */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-1 rounded-xl bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800 p-1">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setInquiryType('email')}
              className={`rounded-lg px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
                inquiryType === 'email'
                  ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-900 shadow-sm'
                  : 'text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-white'
              }`}
            >
              Formal Email Inquiry
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setInquiryType('in_person')}
              className={`rounded-lg px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
                inquiryType === 'in_person'
                  ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-900 shadow-sm'
                  : 'text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-white'
              }`}
            >
              In-Person Desk Brief
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="text-right">
            <span className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400">Target: </span>
            <strong className="text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-white font-semibold">
              {targetAuthority}
            </strong>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Inquiry Text Preview */}
        <div className="relative rounded-xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-zinc-50 p-4 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-850">
          <textarea
            readOnly
            rows={12}
            value={activeText}
            className="w-full resize-none bg-transparent font-mono text-xs leading-relaxed text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200 focus:outline-hidden"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Footer Actions */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-zinc-100 pt-3 dark:border-zinc-800">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleCopy}
              className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1.5 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-850 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 shadow-soft transition-colors"
            >
              {copied ? <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-emerald-500" /> : <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />}
              <span>{copied ? 'Copied to Clipboard!' : 'Copy to Clipboard'}</span>
            </button>

            {inquiryType === 'email' && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleOpenMailClient}
                className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1.5 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-850 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 shadow-soft transition-colors"
              >
                <ExternalLink className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                <span>Open in Email App</span>
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="rounded-xl bg-zinc-900 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-900 dark:hover:bg-zinc-200 shadow-soft"
          >
            Close
          </button>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: frontend/src/components/MetricCardsRow.jsx
================================================
import React from 'react';
import { Layers, AlertTriangle, GitCompare, ShieldCheck } from 'lucide-react';

export default function MetricCardsRow({
  sourcesCount = 0,
  analysis = null,
}) {
  const darkSpotsCount = analysis?.informationGaps
    ? String(analysis.informationGaps.length).padStart(2, '0')
    : sourcesCount > 0
    ? '03'
    : '00';

  const contradictionsCount = analysis?.contradictions
    ? String(analysis.contradictions.length).padStart(2, '0')
    : sourcesCount > 0
    ? '02'
    : '00';

  const readinessDisplay = analysis?.readinessScore !== undefined
    ? `${analysis.readinessScore}/100`
    : sourcesCount > 0
    ? '54/100'
    : '--/100';

  const readinessSubtitle = analysis?.readinessLabel
    ? analysis.readinessLabel.slice(0, 32)
    : 'Requires official verification';

  const metrics = [
    {
      title: 'Collected Sources',
      value: String(sourcesCount).padStart(2, '0'),
      subtitle: 'Cross-verification ready',
      icon: Layers,
    },
    {
      title: 'Potential Dark Spots',
      value: darkSpotsCount,
      subtitle: 'Unanswered policy questions',
      icon: AlertTriangle,
    },
    {
      title: 'Source Contradictions',
      value: contradictionsCount,
      subtitle: 'Conflicting claims flagged',
      icon: GitCompare,
    },
    {
      title: 'Decision Readiness',
      value: readinessDisplay,
      subtitle: readinessSubtitle,
      icon: ShieldCheck,
    },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
      {metrics.map((m, idx) => {
        const Icon = m.icon;
        return (
          <div
            key={idx}
            className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-white p-5 shadow-soft transition-all hover:border-zinc-300 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:hover:border-zinc-700"
          >
            {/* Top row: Title on left, minimal outlined icon on right */}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-xs font-medium text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
                {m.title}
              </span>
              <div className="flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-zinc-200/60 bg-zinc-50 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-800/80 text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                <Icon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Middle row: Large bold number */}
            <div className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight text-zinc-900 dark:text-white">
              {m.value}
            </div>

            {/* Bottom row: Subtle helper subtitle */}
            <div className="mt-2 text-[11px] text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-500 truncate">
              {m.subtitle}
            </div>
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

import React from 'react';
import {
  Radar,
  RotateCcw,
  Sun,
  Moon,
  History,
  Sliders,
  Database,
  Sparkles,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useTheme } from '../context/ThemeContext';

export default function Navbar({
  backendHealth,
  hasOpenRouterKey,
  activeModel,
  onOpenKeyModal,
  onResetWorkspace,
  forceOfflineDemo,
  onOpenSavedSessions,
  currentStep = 1,
  onStepClick,
  hasAnalysis = false,
}) {
  const { theme, toggleTheme } = useTheme();
  const isSupabaseConnected = Boolean(backendHealth?.data?.supabaseConnected);

  // Tab definitions
  const steps = [
    { step: 1, label: '1. Research Goal', accessible: true },
    { step: 2, label: '2. Source Workspace', accessible: true },
    { step: 3, label: '3. Gap & Conflict Dashboard', accessible: hasAnalysis },
    { step: 4, label: '4. Verification Checklist', accessible: hasAnalysis },
  ];

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 w-full border-b border-zinc-200/80 bg-white/95 backdrop-blur-md transition-colors duration-200 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950/95 no-print">
      <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-7xl items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        
        {/* Left: Minimalist square black icon container + GapLens title */}
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-3 shrink-0">
          <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-zinc-900 text-white shadow-sm ring-1 ring-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-900">
            <Radar className="h-5 w-5" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
              <span className="text-base font-bold tracking-tight text-zinc-900 dark:text-white">
                GapLens
              </span>
              <span className="hidden sm:inline-flex items-center rounded-full bg-zinc-100 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium text-zinc-600 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-300">
                <span className="mr-1.5 h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-zinc-400" />
                Audit Engine
              </span>
            </div>
            <p className="hidden md:block text-[11px] text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-500">
              Information Gap &amp; Contradiction Radar
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Center: Horizontal Tab Navigation */}
        <nav
          aria-label="Wizard Steps Navigation"
          className="hidden md:flex items-center space-x-1 rounded-2xl bg-zinc-100/80 p-1 dark:bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-200/60 dark:border-zinc-800"
        >
          {steps.map((s) => {
            const isActive = currentStep === s.step;
            const isAccessible = s.accessible;

            return (
              <button
                key={s.step}
                type="button"
                disabled={!isAccessible}
                onClick={() => isAccessible && onStepClick && onStepClick(s.step)}
                className={`transition-all rounded-xl px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold whitespace-nowrap ${
                  isActive
                    ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-900 shadow-sm'
                    : isAccessible
                    ? 'text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-white'
                    : 'cursor-not-allowed opacity-40 text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-600'
                }`}
              >
                {s.label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </nav>

        {/* Right: Circular / rounded icon buttons */}
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 shrink-0">
          
          {/* Subtle Supabase Connection Dot */}
          <div
            className="hidden xl:inline-flex items-center space-x-1.5 rounded-full bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-200/80 dark:border-zinc-800 px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400"
            title={isSupabaseConnected ? 'Connected to Supabase PostgreSQL' : 'Operating in resilient Local Memory fallback'}
          >
            <span
              className={`h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full ${
                isSupabaseConnected ? 'bg-emerald-500' : 'bg-zinc-400'
              }`}
            />
            <span>{isSupabaseConnected ? 'Supabase' : 'Local'}</span>
          </div>

          {/* Theme Toggle (Sun/Moon) */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={toggleTheme}
            className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-zinc-200/80 bg-white text-zinc-700 shadow-soft hover:bg-zinc-100 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:hover:text-white transition-colors"
            title={theme === 'dark' ? 'Switch to Light Mode' : 'Switch to Dark Mode'}
            aria-label="Toggle theme"
          >
            {theme === 'dark' ? (
              <Sun className="h-4 w-4 text-amber-400" />
            ) : (
              <Moon className="h-4 w-4 text-zinc-700" />
            )}
          </button>

          {/* Saved Sessions Drawer Button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onOpenSavedSessions}
            className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-zinc-200/80 bg-white text-zinc-700 shadow-soft hover:bg-zinc-100 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:hover:text-white transition-colors"
            title="Saved Sessions History"
            aria-label="Saved Sessions"
          >
            <History className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>

          {/* AI Settings / Model Config Modal Button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onOpenKeyModal}
            className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-zinc-200/80 bg-white text-zinc-700 shadow-soft hover:bg-zinc-100 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:hover:text-white transition-colors"
            title="AI Model & API Key Settings"
            aria-label="AI Settings"
          >
            <Sliders className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>

          {/* Reset Workspace */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onResetWorkspace}
            className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-zinc-200/80 bg-white text-zinc-500 shadow-soft hover:bg-zinc-100 hover:text-rose-600 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:hover:text-rose-400 transition-colors"
            title="Reset Workspace"
            aria-label="Reset Workspace"
          >
            <RotateCcw className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>

        </div>

      </div>

      {/* Mobile/Tablet Horizontal Step Tabs */}
      <div className="flex md:hidden overflow-x-auto border-t border-zinc-200/60 dark:border-zinc-800 px-4 py-2 space-x-1.5 scrollbar-none">
        {steps.map((s) => {
          const isActive = currentStep === s.step;
          const isAccessible = s.accessible;

          return (
            <button
              key={s.step}
              type="button"
              disabled={!isAccessible}
              onClick={() => isAccessible && onStepClick && onStepClick(s.step)}
              className={`transition-all rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 ${
                isActive
                  ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-900 shadow-sm'
                  : isAccessible
                  ? 'text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-white bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-900'
                  : 'cursor-not-allowed opacity-40 text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-600'
              }`}
            >
              {s.label}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

FILE: frontend/src/components/RubricCoverageBar.jsx
================================================
import React from 'react';
import {
  CheckCircle,
  AlertTriangle,
  HelpCircle,
  Layers,
  ChevronRight,
  Sparkles,
} from 'lucide-react';

export default function RubricCoverageBar({
  expectedRubric = [],
  rubricStatus = [],
  activeTab = 'gaps',
  onSelectTab,
}) {
  if (!expectedRubric || expectedRubric.length === 0) return null;

  // Build a mapped status list combining expectedRubric and rubricStatus
  const items = expectedRubric.map((param, index) => {
    const found = rubricStatus?.find(
      (r) => (r.parameter || '').toLowerCase() === (param || '').toLowerCase()
    );

    let status = found?.status || 'missing';
    // Fallback heuristic if rubricStatus is not pre-populated
    if (!found) {
      if (index === 0) status = 'verified';
      else if (index === 1 || index === 2) status = 'conflict';
      else status = 'missing';
    }

    return {
      parameter: param,
      status,
      linkedIds: found?.linkedIds || [],
    };
  });

  const getStatusConfig = (status) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'verified':
        return {
          icon: <CheckCircle className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 shrink-0" />,
          label: 'Verified in Sources',
          pillBg:
            'border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50/80 text-emerald-900 hover:bg-emerald-100/80 dark:border-emerald-900/60 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 dark:text-emerald-200 dark:hover:bg-emerald-900/60',
          badgeBg: 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 dark:bg-emerald-900/80 dark:text-emerald-300',
          targetTab: 'facts',
        };
      case 'conflict':
        return {
          icon: <AlertTriangle className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 shrink-0" />,
          label: 'Conflicting Claims',
          pillBg:
            'border-rose-200 bg-rose-50/80 text-rose-900 hover:bg-rose-100/80 dark:border-rose-900/60 dark:bg-rose-950/40 dark:text-rose-200 dark:hover:bg-rose-900/60',
          badgeBg: 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-800 dark:bg-rose-900/80 dark:text-rose-300',
          targetTab: 'conflicts',
        };
      case 'missing':
      default:
        return {
          icon: <HelpCircle className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 shrink-0" />,
          label: 'Digital Dark Spot (Missing)',
          pillBg:
            'border-amber-200 bg-amber-50/80 text-amber-900 hover:bg-amber-100/80 dark:border-amber-900/60 dark:bg-amber-950/40 dark:text-amber-200 dark:hover:bg-amber-900/60',
          badgeBg: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 dark:bg-amber-900/80 dark:text-amber-300',
          targetTab: 'gaps',
        };
    }
  };

  const verifiedCount = items.filter((i) => i.status === 'verified').length;
  const conflictCount = items.filter((i) => i.status === 'conflict').length;
  const missingCount = items.filter((i) => i.status === 'missing').length;

  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-4 shadow-sm transition-colors dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900/90 space-y-3">
      {/* Header with coverage statistics */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800/80 pb-2.5">
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
          <span className="flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 dark:bg-indigo-950 dark:text-indigo-300">
            <Layers className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          </span>
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">
              Decision Completeness Rubric
            </h3>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
              Coverage evaluation across 6 core decision parameters
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Mini stats counters */}
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-[10px] font-semibold">
          <span className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 rounded-md bg-emerald-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200 dark:bg-emerald-950/60 dark:text-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-800">
            <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500" />
            <span>{verifiedCount} Verified</span>
          </span>
          <span className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 rounded-md bg-rose-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-rose-700 border border-rose-200 dark:bg-rose-950/60 dark:text-rose-300 dark:border-rose-800">
            <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-rose-500" />
            <span>{conflictCount} Conflicted</span>
          </span>
          <span className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 rounded-md bg-amber-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-amber-700 border border-amber-200 dark:bg-amber-950/60 dark:text-amber-300 dark:border-amber-800">
            <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-amber-500" />
            <span>{missingCount} Missing</span>
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Grid of Interactive Rubric Pills */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-2.5">
        {items.map((item, idx) => {
          const config = getStatusConfig(item.status);
          const isCurrentTab = activeTab === config.targetTab;

          return (
            <button
              key={idx}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onSelectTab && onSelectTab(config.targetTab)}
              className={`group flex items-start justify-between rounded-xl border p-2.5 text-left transition-all duration-150 ${
                config.pillBg
              } ${isCurrentTab ? 'ring-2 ring-indigo-500/50 shadow-xs' : 'hover:scale-[1.01]'}`}
              title={`Click to view findings under "${config.label}"`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-start space-x-2 min-w-0 pr-1">
                <div className="mt-0.5">{config.icon}</div>
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <span className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 truncate">
                    {item.parameter}
                  </span>
                  <span className="block text-[10px] font-medium opacity-80 mt-0.5">
                    {config.label}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <ChevronRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-slate-400 group-hover:text-slate-700 dark:group-hover:text-slate-200 shrink-0 mt-1 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-0.5" />
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  Sparkles,
  GraduationCap,
  Store,
  Briefcase,
  Check,
} from 'lucide-react';

export default function SampleLoader({ scenarios, onSelectScenario, activeScenarioId }) {
  const [copiedNotification, setCopiedNotification] = useState(null);

  const handleSelect = (scenario) => {
    onSelectScenario(scenario);
    setCopiedNotification(scenario.id);
    setTimeout(() => {
      setCopiedNotification(null);
    }, 2000);
  };

  const getCategoryIcon = (category) => {
    switch (category) {
      case 'Education':
        return GraduationCap;
      case 'Business':
        return Store;
      case 'Careers':
      default:
        return Briefcase;
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-white p-4 sm:p-5 shadow-soft dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 transition-colors">
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between">
        
        {/* Left explanation */}
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <span className="flex h-5 w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-900">
              <Sparkles className="h-3 w-3" />
            </span>
            <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-900 dark:text-white">
              Pre-Configured Evaluation Scenarios
            </span>
            <span className="rounded-full bg-zinc-100 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium text-zinc-600 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-400">
              3 Test Sets
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 max-w-2xl">
            Pre-seeded test scenarios with intentional regulatory gaps, contradicting thresholds, and temporal drift.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Right scenario buttons */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2.5">
          {scenarios && scenarios.map((scenario) => {
            const isActive = activeScenarioId === scenario.id;
            const Icon = getCategoryIcon(scenario.category);

            return (
              <button
                key={scenario.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleSelect(scenario)}
                className={`group relative flex flex-col justify-between rounded-xl border p-3 text-left transition-all ${
                  isActive
                    ? 'border-zinc-900 bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:border-white dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-900 shadow-sm'
                    : 'border-zinc-200/80 bg-zinc-50 text-zinc-700 hover:border-zinc-300 hover:bg-white dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-850/60 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:border-zinc-700 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800'
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex items-start space-x-2.5">
                  <div
                    className={`flex h-7 w-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg mt-0.5 ${
                      isActive
                        ? 'bg-zinc-800 text-white dark:bg-zinc-200 dark:text-zinc-900'
                        : 'bg-zinc-200 text-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-300'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <Icon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                  </div>

                  <div className="text-left flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 justify-between">
                      <span className="text-xs font-bold leading-tight truncate">
                        {scenario.name}
                      </span>
                      {isActive && (
                        <span className="flex h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white text-zinc-900 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-white">
                          <Check className="h-2.5 w-2.5 stroke-[3]" />
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <p
                      className={`text-[10px] mt-0.5 line-clamp-1 ${
                        isActive ? 'text-zinc-300 dark:text-zinc-600' : 'text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-500'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {scenario.category} &bull; {(scenario.sources || []).length} Sources
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {copiedNotification === scenario.id && (
                  <div className="mt-2 text-[10px] font-semibold text-emerald-400">
                    Loaded Scenario
                  </div>
                )}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
