FILE: frontend/src/components/SavedSessionsDrawer.jsx
================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  X,
  History,
  Trash2,
  ExternalLink,
  FolderOpen,
  Calendar,
  Layers,
  ArrowRight,
  Database,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { fetchSavedSessions, deleteSavedSession } from '../services/api';

export default function SavedSessionsDrawer({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onLoadSession,
}) {
  const [sessions, setSessions] = useState([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [storageType, setStorageType] = useState('supabase');

  const loadSessionsList = async () => {
    setIsLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetchSavedSessions();
      setSessions(res.sessions || []);
      setStorageType(res.storage || 'localStorage');
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('[SavedSessionsDrawer] Error loading sessions:', err);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      loadSessionsList();
    }
  }, [isOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape' && isOpen) {
        onClose();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [isOpen, onClose]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    } else {
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
    }
    return () => {
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
    };
  }, [isOpen]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleDelete = async (e, sessionId) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (window.confirm('Delete this saved research session?')) {
      await deleteSavedSession(sessionId);
      setSessions((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s.id !== sessionId));
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 overflow-hidden no-print">
      <div
        className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-xs transition-opacity"
        onClick={onClose}
      />

      <div className="fixed inset-y-0 right-0 flex max-w-full pl-10">
        <div className="w-screen max-w-md transform border-l border-zinc-200/80 bg-white shadow-2xl transition-all dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 flex flex-col">
          
          {/* Header */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-zinc-100 px-6 py-4 dark:border-zinc-800">
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2.5">
              <span className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-900">
                <History className="h-4 w-4" />
              </span>
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white">
                  Saved Research Analyses
                </h3>
                <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 text-[11px] text-zinc-400">
                  <Database className="h-3 w-3" />
                  <span>Storage: {storageType === 'supabase' ? 'Supabase Table' : 'Local Storage'}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="rounded-full p-1.5 text-zinc-400 hover:bg-zinc-100 hover:text-zinc-700 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:hover:text-zinc-200"
            >
              <X className="h-4 w-4" />
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* List Body */}
          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-6 py-4 space-y-3">
            {isLoading && (
              <div className="py-12 text-center text-xs text-zinc-400">
                Loading saved sessions...
              </div>
            )}

            {!isLoading && sessions.length === 0 && (
              <div className="py-12 text-center text-xs text-zinc-400 space-y-2">
                <FolderOpen className="mx-auto h-8 w-8 text-zinc-300 dark:text-zinc-700" />
                <p>No saved analyses found.</p>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500">
                  Run an audit in Step 2 to automatically save your sessions.
                </p>
              </div>
            )}

            {!isLoading &&
              sessions.map((session) => {
                const analysis = session.analysis_result || session.analysisResult;
                const score = analysis?.readinessScore;
                const sourceCount = (session.sources || []).length;
                const dateFormatted = session.created_at
                  ? new Date(session.created_at).toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
                      month: 'short',
                      day: 'numeric',
                      hour: '2-digit',
                      minute: '2-digit',
                    })
                  : 'Recent';

                return (
                  <div
                    key={session.id}
                    onClick={() => {
                      onLoadSession(session);
                      onClose();
                    }}
                    className="group cursor-pointer rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-zinc-50/60 p-4 transition-all hover:border-zinc-300 hover:bg-white dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-850/50 dark:hover:border-zinc-700 dark:hover:bg-zinc-850 space-y-2 shadow-soft"
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                      <span className="rounded-full bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
                        {session.category || 'General'}
                      </span>
                      {score !== undefined && (
                        <span className="font-mono text-xs font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white">
                          {score}/100
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white line-clamp-2 leading-snug">
                      {session.topic}
                    </h4>

                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[10px] text-zinc-400 pt-1 border-t border-zinc-200/50 dark:border-zinc-800">
                      <span>{dateFormatted} &bull; {sourceCount} sources</span>
                      <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={(e) => handleDelete(e, session.id)}
                          className="p-1 text-zinc-400 hover:text-rose-600"
                          title="Delete session"
                        >
                          <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        </button>
                        <span className="font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white flex items-center space-x-0.5 group-hover:translate-x-0.5 transition-transform">
                          <span>Open</span>
                          <ArrowRight className="h-3 w-3" />
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
          </div>

          {/* Footer */}
          <div className="border-t border-zinc-100 bg-zinc-50 px-6 py-3.5 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950 flex justify-end">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="rounded-xl bg-zinc-900 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-900 shadow-soft"
            >
              Close
            </button>
          </div>

        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



================================================
FILE: frontend/src/components/SearchSourcesModal.jsx
================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  X,
  Search,
  Globe,
  ExternalLink,
  Plus,
  Loader2,
  CheckCircle2,
  AlertCircle,
  Sparkles,
  Layers
} from 'lucide-react';
import { searchWebSources } from '../services/api';

export default function SearchSourcesModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  initialQuery = '',
  onImportSource,
  canAddMore = true,
}) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState(initialQuery);
  const [searching, setSearching] = useState(false);
  const [results, setResults] = useState([]);
  const [importedUrls, setImportedUrls] = useState([]);
  const [hasSearched, setHasSearched] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      const q = initialQuery.trim() || 'Post-Matric Scholarship guidelines';
      setQuery(q);
      handleExecuteSearch(q);
    }
  }, [isOpen, initialQuery]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleExecuteSearch = async (searchTerm) => {
    const q = (searchTerm || query).trim();
    if (!q) return;

    setSearching(true);
    setHasSearched(true);
    const searchData = await searchWebSources(q);
    setResults(searchData);
    setSearching(false);
  };

  const handleImport = (item, shouldExtract = true) => {
    if (!canAddMore) return;
    onImportSource({
      title: item.title,
      url: item.url,
      content: item.snippet || '',
      sourceType: item.suggestedSourceType || 'Third-Party Guide',
      autoExtract: shouldExtract,
    });
    setImportedUrls((prev) => [...prev, item.url]);
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-950/70 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div className="relative w-full max-w-2xl max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-2xl transition-colors duration-200 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900 sm:p-6 p-4">
        
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 pb-3">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2.5">
            <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-600 ring-1 ring-indigo-200 dark:bg-indigo-950 dark:text-indigo-400 dark:ring-indigo-800">
              <Globe className="h-4 w-4" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                Permitted Web Source Search
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                Discover candidate notices, guidance blogs, and circulars to feed into GapLens
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="rounded-lg p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-700 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 dark:hover:text-white transition-colors"
          >
            <X className="h-5 w-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Search Bar */}
        <div className="mt-4">
          <form
            onSubmit={(e) => {
              e.preventDefault();
              handleExecuteSearch(query);
            }}
            className="flex items-center space-x-2"
          >
            <div className="relative flex-1">
              <input
                type="text"
                value={query}
                onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Search policy circulars, scheme guidelines, or licensing portals..."
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-4 py-2.5 pl-10 text-xs text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 focus:border-indigo-500 focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500/20 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-950 dark:text-slate-100 dark:placeholder-slate-500 dark:focus:bg-slate-950"
              />
              <Search className="absolute left-3.5 top-3 h-4 w-4 text-slate-400" />
            </div>
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={searching || !query.trim()}
              className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center space-x-1.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white shadow-sm hover:bg-indigo-500 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors"
            >
              {searching ? (
                <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin text-white" />
              ) : (
                <Search className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              )}
              <span>{searching ? 'Searching...' : 'Search'}</span>
            </button>
          </form>
          {!canAddMore && (
            <div className="mt-2 text-[11px] text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
              Workspace full (6 of 6 sources loaded). Remove a source to import more.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Results Container */}
        <div className="mt-4 flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-3 pr-1 min-h-[220px]">
          {searching ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-12 text-slate-400">
              <Loader2 className="h-7 w-7 animate-spin text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 mb-2" />
              <p className="text-xs">Querying permitted portals & indexes...</p>
            </div>
          ) : results.length > 0 ? (
            results.map((item, idx) => {
              const isImported = importedUrls.includes(item.url);
              return (
                <div
                  key={idx}
                  className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/70 p-3.5 transition-all hover:border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-950/70 dark:hover:border-slate-700"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                    <div className="space-y-1">
                      <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                        <span className="rounded bg-indigo-100 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold text-indigo-700 dark:bg-indigo-950 dark:text-indigo-300">
                          {item.suggestedSourceType || 'Web Source'}
                        </span>
                        <a
                          href={item.url}
                          target="_blank"
                          rel="noreferrer"
                          className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 hover:text-indigo-600 dark:text-white dark:hover:text-indigo-400 inline-flex items-center gap-1"
                        >
                          <span>{item.title}</span>
                          <ExternalLink className="h-3 w-3 shrink-0 opacity-70" />
                        </a>
                      </div>
                      <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
                        {item.snippet}
                      </p>
                      <div className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 truncate max-w-md">
                        {item.url}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Import Buttons */}
                    <div className="shrink-0 flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                      {isImported ? (
                        <span className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 rounded-lg bg-emerald-100 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-800 dark:bg-emerald-950 dark:text-emerald-300">
                          <CheckCircle2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400" />
                          <span>Imported</span>
                        </span>
                      ) : (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          disabled={!canAddMore}
                          onClick={() => handleImport(item, true)}
                          className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 rounded-lg bg-indigo-600 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white shadow-sm hover:bg-indigo-500 disabled:opacity-40 transition-colors"
                          title="Import into workspace and automatically scrape its full text"
                        >
                          <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                          <span>+ Import as Source</span>
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })
          ) : hasSearched ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-10 text-center text-slate-400">
              <AlertCircle className="h-6 w-6 text-slate-400 mb-1" />
              <p className="text-xs">No matching permitted sources found.</p>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Try searching with broader keywords.</p>
            </div>
          ) : null}
        </div>

        {/* Footer */}
        <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 pt-3">
          <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <Sparkles className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-indigo-500" />
            <span>Permitted indexing protects against hallucinated URLs</span>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:text-slate-300 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
          >
            Close
          </button>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}




================================================
FILE: frontend/src/components/SourceCard.jsx
================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  FileText,
  Trash2,
  Sparkles,
  Loader2,
  Clock,
  FileUp,
  CalendarSearch,
  Check,
  AlertTriangle,
  ExternalLink,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { extractUrlContent, scanTextMetadata } from '../services/api';

const SOURCE_TYPES = [
  'Official Notice',
  'Third-Party Guide',
  'Archived Circular',
  'News Article',
  'Forum/Other',
];

export default function SourceCard({
  source,
  index,
  totalSources,
  onUpdateSource,
  onDeleteSource,
}) {
  const [extracting, setExtracting] = useState(false);
  const [extractError, setExtractError] = useState(null);
  const [extractSuccess, setExtractSuccess] = useState(null);
  const [detectingDate, setDetectingDate] = useState(false);
  const [temporalInfo, setTemporalInfo] = useState({
    temporalStatus: 'undated',
    detectedDate: '',
  });

  const fileInputRef = useRef(null);

  const wordCount = source.content ? source.content.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length : 0;
  const charCount = source.content?.length || 0;

  // Run initial or debounced temporal detection on content/date change
  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    const runScan = async () => {
      if (source.content && source.content.trim().length > 15) {
        const res = await scanTextMetadata(source.content, source.publishedDate);
        if (active) {
          setTemporalInfo({
            temporalStatus: res.temporalStatus,
            detectedDate: res.detectedDate || source.publishedDate || '',
          });
        }
      } else {
        const hasDate = Boolean(source.publishedDate && source.publishedDate.trim());
        if (active) {
          setTemporalInfo({
            temporalStatus: hasDate ? (source.publishedDate.includes('2023') || source.publishedDate.includes('2021') ? 'outdated' : 'current') : 'undated',
            detectedDate: source.publishedDate || '',
          });
        }
      }
    };
    runScan();
    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [source.content, source.publishedDate]);

  const handleFieldChange = (field, val) => {
    onUpdateSource(source.id, { [field]: val });
  };

  // Auto-Extract URL content with Jina AI / Cheerio / Firewall guard
  const handleExtractUrl = async () => {
    if (!source.url || !source.url.trim()) {
      setExtractError('Please enter a valid URL first (e.g. https://...).');
      return;
    }

    setExtracting(true);
    setExtractError(null);
    setExtractSuccess(null);

    const result = await extractUrlContent(source.url.trim());
    setExtracting(false);

    if (result.success && result.content) {
      handleFieldChange('content', result.content);
      if (result.title && (!source.title || source.title.startsWith('Source '))) {
        handleFieldChange('title', result.title);
      }
      if (result.detectedDate && !source.publishedDate) {
        handleFieldChange('publishedDate', result.detectedDate);
      }
      setExtractSuccess(`Extracted ${result.content.length} characters.`);
      setTimeout(() => setExtractSuccess(null), 4000);
    } else {
      setExtractError(result.message || 'Failed to extract web content.');
    }
  };

  // Local File Upload (.txt or .md)
  const handleFileUpload = (e) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;

    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (event) => {
      const text = event.target?.result;
      if (typeof text === 'string') {
        handleFieldChange('content', text);
        const fileNameWithoutExt = file.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '');
        if (!source.title || source.title.startsWith('Source ')) {
          handleFieldChange('title', fileNameWithoutExt);
        }
        setExtractSuccess(`Loaded file "${file.name}" (${text.length} characters)`);
        setTimeout(() => setExtractSuccess(null), 3000);
      }
    };
    reader.readAsText(file);
    e.target.value = '';
  };

  // Auto-Detect Date
  const handleAutoDetectDate = async () => {
    if (!source.content) return;
    setDetectingDate(true);
    const res = await scanTextMetadata(source.content, source.publishedDate);
    setDetectingDate(false);

    if (res.detectedDate) {
      handleFieldChange('publishedDate', res.detectedDate);
      setTemporalInfo({
        temporalStatus: res.temporalStatus,
        detectedDate: res.detectedDate,
      });
      setExtractSuccess(`Detected date: ${res.detectedDate}`);
      setTimeout(() => setExtractSuccess(null), 3000);
    } else {
      setExtractError('No dates detected in text.');
      setTimeout(() => setExtractError(null), 3000);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-white p-5 shadow-soft dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 transition-colors">
      
      {/* Top Bar: Source ID, Type, Date Status, Upload & Delete */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 pb-3">
        
        {/* Source ID and Type */}
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
          <span className="flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-900 text-xs font-mono font-bold">
            {index + 1}
          </span>
          <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white">
            {source.id || `src-${index + 1}`}
          </span>

          {/* Temporal status dot badge */}
          {temporalInfo.temporalStatus === 'undated' && (
            <span className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 rounded-full bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 border border-zinc-200/60 dark:border-zinc-700/60">
              <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-amber-500" />
              <span>Undated</span>
            </span>
          )}

          {temporalInfo.temporalStatus === 'outdated' && (
            <span className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 rounded-full bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 border border-zinc-200/60 dark:border-zinc-700/60">
              <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-rose-500" />
              <span>Outdated Cycle</span>
            </span>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Type Select, File Upload, Delete Action */}
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
          <select
            value={source.sourceType}
            onChange={(e) => handleFieldChange('sourceType', e.target.value)}
            className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-zinc-50 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-800 focus:border-zinc-900 focus:outline-hidden dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200"
          >
            {SOURCE_TYPES.map((type) => (
              <option key={type} value={type}>
                {type}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>

          {/* Upload .txt/.md File Button */}
          <input
            type="file"
            ref={fileInputRef}
            onChange={handleFileUpload}
            accept=".txt,.md"
            className="hidden"
          />
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
            title="Upload local .txt or .md document"
            className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-zinc-50 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:bg-zinc-750 transition-colors"
          >
            <FileUp className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400" />
            <span className="hidden sm:inline">Upload File</span>
          </button>

          {/* Delete Source button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={totalSources <= 2}
            onClick={() => onDeleteSource(source.id)}
            title={
              totalSources <= 2
                ? 'Minimum 2 sources required for cross-verification'
                : 'Remove this source'
            }
            className={`rounded-xl p-1.5 transition-colors ${
              totalSources <= 2
                ? 'cursor-not-allowed text-zinc-300 dark:text-zinc-700'
                : 'text-zinc-400 hover:bg-zinc-100 hover:text-rose-600 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:hover:text-rose-400'
            }`}
          >
            <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

      </div>

      {/* Grid Inputs: Title, Date/Cycle, URL + Auto-Extract */}
      <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-1 gap-3.5 sm:grid-cols-12">
        
        {/* Source Title */}
        <div className="sm:col-span-5">
          <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-500 mb-1">
            Source Title
          </label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={source.title}
            onChange={(e) => handleFieldChange('title', e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Official State Portal"
            className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 placeholder-zinc-400 focus:border-zinc-900 focus:bg-white focus:outline-hidden font-medium dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-850 dark:text-zinc-100 dark:placeholder-zinc-600 dark:focus:border-white"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Publication Date / Cycle + Auto-Detect Button */}
        <div className="sm:col-span-3">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
            <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-500">
              Date / Cycle
            </label>
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={detectingDate || !source.content}
              onClick={handleAutoDetectDate}
              title="Auto-scan pasted content to detect dates"
              className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 text-[10px] font-semibold text-zinc-700 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:text-white disabled:opacity-40 transition-colors"
            >
              {detectingDate ? (
                <Loader2 className="h-2.5 w-2.5 animate-spin" />
              ) : (
                <CalendarSearch className="h-2.5 w-2.5" />
              )}
              <span>Detect</span>
            </button>
          </div>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={source.publishedDate}
            onChange={(e) => handleFieldChange('publishedDate', e.target.value)}
            placeholder="e.g. Sept 2026, 2023, or blank"
            className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-mono text-zinc-900 placeholder-zinc-400 focus:border-zinc-900 focus:outline-hidden dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-850 dark:text-zinc-100 dark:placeholder-zinc-600 dark:focus:border-white"
          />
        </div>

        {/* URL Input + Auto-Extract Button */}
        <div className="sm:col-span-4">
          <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-500 mb-1">
            Source URL
          </label>
          <div className="flex space-x-1.5">
            <input
              type="url"
              value={source.url}
              onChange={(e) => handleFieldChange('url', e.target.value)}
              placeholder="https://..."
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-mono text-zinc-900 placeholder-zinc-400 focus:border-zinc-900 focus:outline-hidden dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-850 dark:text-zinc-100 dark:placeholder-zinc-600 dark:focus:border-white"
            />
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={extracting || !source.url}
              onClick={handleExtractUrl}
              title="Scrape readable text automatically from this URL"
              className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 rounded-xl bg-zinc-900 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white hover:bg-zinc-800 disabled:opacity-40 transition-all shrink-0 dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-900 dark:hover:bg-zinc-200 shadow-soft"
            >
              {extracting ? (
                <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" />
              ) : (
                <Sparkles className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              )}
              <span className="hidden md:inline">Extract</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

      </div>

      {/* Extraction Notifications */}
      {extractError && (
        <div className="mt-2.5 flex items-center space-x-2 rounded-xl bg-zinc-50 p-2 text-xs text-rose-600 border border-zinc-200/60 dark:bg-zinc-850 dark:text-rose-400 dark:border-zinc-800">
          <AlertTriangle className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
          <span>{extractError}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {extractSuccess && (
        <div className="mt-2.5 flex items-center space-x-2 rounded-xl bg-zinc-50 p-2 text-xs text-emerald-600 border border-zinc-200/60 dark:bg-zinc-850 dark:text-emerald-400 dark:border-zinc-800">
          <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
          <span>{extractSuccess}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Content Textarea */}
      <div className="mt-3.5">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
          <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-500">
            Source Text Extract (Evidence Corpus)
          </label>
          <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-zinc-400">
            {wordCount} words &bull; {charCount} chars
          </span>
        </div>
        <textarea
          rows={4}
          value={source.content}
          onChange={(e) => handleFieldChange('content', e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Paste or extract regulatory text, guidelines, conditions, or eligibility criteria here..."
          className="w-full resize-y rounded-xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-zinc-50 p-3 text-xs leading-relaxed text-zinc-800 placeholder-zinc-400 focus:border-zinc-900 focus:bg-white focus:outline-hidden dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-850 dark:text-zinc-100 dark:placeholder-zinc-600 dark:focus:border-white transition-all font-mono"
        />
      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

FILE: frontend/src/components/SourceCollector.jsx
================================================
import React from 'react';
import {
  Layers,
  Plus,
  FileCheck,
  CalendarClock,
} from 'lucide-react';
import SourceCard from './SourceCard';

export default function SourceCollector({
  sources,
  onAddSource,
  onUpdateSource,
  onDeleteSource,
}) {
  const maxSources = 6;
  const count = sources.length;
  const canAdd = count < maxSources;

  const validSourcesCount = sources.filter(
    (s) => s.content && s.content.trim().length >= 10
  ).length;

  const undatedCount = sources.filter(
    (s) => !s.publishedDate || !s.publishedDate.trim()
  ).length;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      
      {/* Section Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-white p-5 shadow-soft dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 transition-colors sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
        <div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <span className="flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-900">
              <Layers className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            </span>
            <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white tracking-tight">
              Collected Sources ({count}/{maxSources}) â€” Min 2 required for cross-verification
            </h2>
          </div>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
            Compare official notices, application portals, circulars, and secondary guidance to reveal dark spots.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Source Status Pills & Add Button */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          {/* Active sources with text */}
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 rounded-full bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 border border-zinc-200/50 dark:border-zinc-700/50">
            <span className={`h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full ${validSourcesCount >= 2 ? 'bg-emerald-500' : 'bg-amber-500'}`} />
            <span>{validSourcesCount}/{count} with text</span>
          </div>

          {/* Undated warnings */}
          {undatedCount > 0 && (
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 rounded-full bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 border border-zinc-200/50 dark:border-zinc-700/50">
              <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-amber-500" />
              <span>{undatedCount} undated</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Add source button in header */}
          {canAdd && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onAddSource}
              className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1.5 rounded-xl bg-zinc-900 px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-900 dark:hover:bg-zinc-200 transition-all shadow-soft"
            >
              <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              <span>+ Add Source</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Render Individual Source Cards */}
      <div className="space-y-4">
        {sources.map((source, index) => (
          <SourceCard
            key={source.id}
            source={source}
            index={index}
            totalSources={sources.length}
            onUpdateSource={onUpdateSource}
            onDeleteSource={onDeleteSource}
          />
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Bottom Add Source Button */}
      {canAdd ? (
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onAddSource}
          className="group flex w-full items-center justify-center space-x-2 rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed border-zinc-300 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white/50 p-4 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-600 hover:border-zinc-900 hover:bg-white hover:text-zinc-900 transition-all dark:bg-zinc-900/40 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:border-zinc-600 dark:hover:bg-zinc-850 dark:hover:text-white"
        >
          <div className="flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-zinc-200 text-zinc-700 group-hover:bg-zinc-900 group-hover:text-white transition-colors dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:group-hover:bg-white dark:group-hover:text-zinc-900">
            <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
          </div>
          <span>+ Add Another Source ({count} of {maxSources} loaded)</span>
        </button>
      ) : (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-100 p-3 text-center text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-850 dark:text-zinc-400">
          Maximum of {maxSources} sources reached for this cross-verification session.
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
}



================================================
FILE: frontend/src/components/SourceInspectorDrawer.jsx
================================================
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
  X,
  ExternalLink,
  Copy,
  Check,
  FileText,
  AlertTriangle,
  GitCompare,
  Clock,
  HelpCircle,
} from 'lucide-react';

function HighlightedContent({ content, quote, highlightClass }) {
  if (!content) {
    return <span className="text-zinc-400 italic">No content available for this source.</span>;
  }

  if (!quote || typeof quote !== 'string' || !quote.trim()) {
    return <span className="whitespace-pre-wrap">{content}</span>;
  }

  const cleanQuote = quote.trim();
  const lowerContent = content.toLowerCase();
  const lowerQuote = cleanQuote.toLowerCase();

  let matchIndex = lowerContent.indexOf(lowerQuote);
  let matchLength = cleanQuote.length;

  if (matchIndex === -1) {
    const words = cleanQuote.split(/\s+/).slice(0, 8).join(' ').toLowerCase();
    if (words.length > 15) {
      matchIndex = lowerContent.indexOf(words);
      if (matchIndex !== -1) {
        matchLength = words.length;
      }
    }
  }

  if (matchIndex === -1) {
    const shortWords = cleanQuote.split(/\s+/).slice(0, 5).join(' ').toLowerCase();
    if (shortWords.length > 10) {
      matchIndex = lowerContent.indexOf(shortWords);
      if (matchIndex !== -1) {
        matchLength = shortWords.length;
      }
    }
  }

  if (matchIndex === -1) {
    return <span className="whitespace-pre-wrap">{content}</span>;
  }

  const before = content.slice(0, matchIndex);
  const match = content.slice(matchIndex, matchIndex + matchLength);
  const after = content.slice(matchIndex + matchLength);

  const defaultHighlight =
    'bg-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-700 text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-semibold border-l-4 border-zinc-900 dark:border-zinc-100 inline shadow-2xs';

  return (
    <span className="whitespace-pre-wrap leading-relaxed">
      {before}
      <mark className={highlightClass || defaultHighlight}>
        {match}
      </mark>
      {after}
    </span>
  );
}

export default function SourceInspectorDrawer({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  mode = 'single',
  source = null,
  evidenceQuote = '',
  reason = 'Supporting Evidence for Verified Fact',
  reasonType = 'fact',
  sourceA = null,
  sourceB = null,
  evidenceQuoteA = '',
  evidenceQuoteB = '',
  disputedParameter = '',
  severity = '',
  resolutionAdvice = '',
  sources = [],
  gapTitle = '',
  missingQuestion = '',
}) {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape' && isOpen) {
        onClose();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [isOpen, onClose]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    } else {
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
    }
    return () => {
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
    };
  }, [isOpen]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const isDiff = mode === 'diff';
  const isAll = mode === 'all';

  const handleCopyQuote = (text) => {
    if (!text) return;
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    setCopied(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 overflow-hidden no-print">
      <div
        className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-xs transition-opacity"
        onClick={onClose}
      />

      <div className="fixed inset-y-0 right-0 flex max-w-full pl-10">
        <div
          className={`w-screen ${
            isDiff ? 'max-w-4xl' : 'max-w-2xl'
          } transform border-l border-zinc-200/80 bg-white shadow-2xl transition-all dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 flex flex-col`}
        >
          {/* Top Header */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-zinc-100 px-6 py-4 dark:border-zinc-800">
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
              <span className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-900">
                {isDiff ? (
                  <GitCompare className="h-4 w-4" />
                ) : isAll ? (
                  <HelpCircle className="h-4 w-4" />
                ) : (
                  <FileText className="h-4 w-4" />
                )}
              </span>
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-sm sm:text-base font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white">
                  {isDiff
                    ? 'Side-by-Side Conflict Diff'
                    : isAll
                    ? 'Cross-Source Absence Verification'
                    : 'Source Evidence Inspector'}
                </h3>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400">
                  {isDiff
                    ? 'Compare conflicting claims in original context'
                    : isAll
                    ? 'Verifying absence of required parameters across sources'
                    : 'Trace highlighted citations directly to raw source material'}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="rounded-full p-2 text-zinc-400 hover:bg-zinc-100 hover:text-zinc-700 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:hover:text-zinc-200 transition-colors"
              title="Close (Esc)"
            >
              <X className="h-5 w-5" />
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Drawer Body */}
          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-6 py-5 space-y-5">
            {/* VIEW MODE 1: DIFF */}
            {isDiff && (
              <div className="space-y-5">
                <div className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-zinc-50 p-4 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-850">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-2">
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-900 dark:text-white flex items-center space-x-1.5">
                      <GitCompare className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                      <span>Disputed Parameter</span>
                    </span>
                    <span className="rounded-full bg-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-700 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200">
                      {severity || 'Conflict'}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <h4 className="text-base font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white">
                    {disputedParameter || 'Contradictory Threshold'}
                  </h4>
                  {resolutionAdvice && (
                    <div className="mt-3 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-3 text-xs text-zinc-700 dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-300">
                      <strong className="font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white">
                        Resolution Advice:{' '}
                      </strong>
                      {resolutionAdvice}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* 2-Column Split */}
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                  {/* Source A */}
                  <div className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-zinc-50/60 p-4 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-850/50 flex flex-col">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-zinc-200/60 dark:border-zinc-800 pb-2.5 mb-3">
                      <span className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white truncate">
                        {sourceA?.title || 'Source A'}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-zinc-400">
                        {sourceA?.publishedDate || 'Undated'}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    {evidenceQuoteA && (
                      <div className="mb-3 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-2.5 text-xs text-zinc-800 dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-200">
                        <span className="font-bold block text-[10px] uppercase text-zinc-500 mb-0.5">
                          Extracted Claim (Source A):
                        </span>
                        "{evidenceQuoteA}"
                      </div>
                    )}

                    <div className="flex-1 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-3 text-xs text-zinc-700 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-300 max-h-96 overflow-y-auto leading-relaxed shadow-inner">
                      <HighlightedContent
                        content={sourceA?.content}
                        quote={evidenceQuoteA}
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Source B */}
                  <div className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-zinc-50/60 p-4 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-850/50 flex flex-col">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-zinc-200/60 dark:border-zinc-800 pb-2.5 mb-3">
                      <span className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white truncate">
                        {sourceB?.title || 'Source B'}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-zinc-400">
                        {sourceB?.publishedDate || 'Undated'}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    {evidenceQuoteB && (
                      <div className="mb-3 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-2.5 text-xs text-zinc-800 dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-200">
                        <span className="font-bold block text-[10px] uppercase text-zinc-500 mb-0.5">
                          Extracted Claim (Source B):
                        </span>
                        "{evidenceQuoteB}"
                      </div>
                    )}

                    <div className="flex-1 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-3 text-xs text-zinc-700 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-300 max-h-96 overflow-y-auto leading-relaxed shadow-inner">
                      <HighlightedContent
                        content={sourceB?.content}
                        quote={evidenceQuoteB}
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* VIEW MODE 2: ALL SOURCES ABSENCE */}
            {isAll && (
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <div className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-zinc-50 p-4 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-850">
                  <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-900 dark:text-white mb-1">
                    <AlertTriangle className="h-4 w-4" />
                    <span>Digital Dark Spot Verification</span>
                  </div>
                  <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white">
                    {gapTitle || 'Missing Requirement'}
                  </h4>
                  <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200/60 dark:border-zinc-800 font-medium">
                    <strong className="text-zinc-900 dark:text-white">Unanswered Question: </strong>
                    {missingQuestion}
                  </p>
                  <p className="mt-2 text-[11px] text-zinc-400">
                    GapLens audited all {sources.length} submitted sources below. None provide verified regulatory instructions for this parameter.
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="space-y-3">
                  {sources.map((s, idx) => (
                    <div
                      key={s.id || idx}
                      className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-zinc-50/60 p-4 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-850/50"
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-zinc-200/60 dark:border-zinc-800 pb-2 mb-2">
                        <span className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white">
                          [{s.id || `src-${idx + 1}`}] {s.title}
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-zinc-400">
                          {s.publishedDate || 'Undated'}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 max-h-36 overflow-y-auto leading-relaxed bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200/80 dark:border-zinc-800 font-mono">
                        {s.content || '(No text recorded)'}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* VIEW MODE 3: SINGLE SOURCE */}
            {!isDiff && !isAll && source && (
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <div className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-zinc-50 p-3.5 text-xs dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-850">
                  <span className="font-bold block text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-900 dark:text-white mb-0.5">
                    Inspection Context
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-300">{reason}</span>
                </div>

                <div className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-zinc-50/60 p-4 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-850/50 space-y-2">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                    <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white">
                      [{source.id || 'src'}] {source.title || 'Referenced Document'}
                    </h4>

                    {source.url && (
                      <a
                        href={source.url}
                        target="_blank"
                        rel="noreferrer"
                        className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-900 hover:underline dark:text-white"
                      >
                        <span>Open URL</span>
                        <ExternalLink className="h-3 w-3" />
                      </a>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 pt-1 text-[11px]">
                    <span className="rounded-full bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 font-medium">
                      Type: {source.sourceType || 'Document'}
                    </span>
                    <span className="rounded-full bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 font-mono">
                      Date: {source.publishedDate || 'Undated'}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {evidenceQuote && (
                  <div className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-zinc-50 p-3.5 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-850">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1.5">
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-900 dark:text-white flex items-center space-x-1">
                        <FileText className="h-3 w-3" />
                        <span>Exact Sourced Quote</span>
                      </span>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleCopyQuote(evidenceQuote)}
                        className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 text-[10px] font-medium text-zinc-600 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-white"
                      >
                        {copied ? <Check className="h-3 w-3 text-emerald-500" /> : <Copy className="h-3 w-3" />}
                        <span>{copied ? 'Copied' : 'Copy Quote'}</span>
                      </button>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-xs font-medium italic text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200 leading-relaxed">
                      "{evidenceQuote}"
                    </p>
                  </div>
                )}

                <div>
                  <h5 className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white mb-2">
                    Raw Source Corpus
                  </h5>
                  <div className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-4 text-xs text-zinc-800 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-200 max-h-[500px] overflow-y-auto font-mono leading-relaxed shadow-inner">
                    <HighlightedContent
                      content={source.content}
                      quote={evidenceQuote}
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Drawer Footer */}
          <div className="border-t border-zinc-100 bg-zinc-50 px-6 py-3.5 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950 flex justify-end">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="rounded-xl bg-zinc-900 px-5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-900 dark:hover:bg-zinc-200 shadow-soft"
            >
              Done Inspecting
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: frontend/src/components/StepProgressBar.jsx

import React from 'react';
import { Target, Layers, Sparkles, ListChecks, Check } from 'lucide-react';

const STEPS = [
  {
    step: 1,
    title: 'Research Goal',
    subtitle: 'Define query & category',
    icon: Target,
  },
  {
    step: 2,
    title: 'Source Workspace',
    subtitle: 'Extract text & verify dates',
    icon: Layers,
  },
  {
    step: 3,
    title: 'Gap & Conflict Dashboard',
    subtitle: 'Inspect dark spots & contradictions',
    icon: Sparkles,
  },
  {
    step: 4,
    title: 'Verification Checklist',
    subtitle: 'Action items & official inquiry',
    icon: ListChecks,
  },
];

export default function StepProgressBar({
  currentStep,
  onStepClick,
  hasAnalysis = false,
  sourcesCount = 0,
}) {
  return (
    <nav
      aria-label="Wizard Steps Progress"
      className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-white p-3 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 shadow-soft"
    >
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-4 sm:gap-3">
        {STEPS.map((s) => {
          const Icon = s.icon;
          const isActive = currentStep === s.step;
          const isCompleted = currentStep > s.step || (s.step <= 2 && hasAnalysis);
          const isAccessible =
            s.step === 1 ||
            s.step === 2 ||
            (s.step === 3 && hasAnalysis) ||
            (s.step === 4 && hasAnalysis);

          return (
            <button
              key={s.step}
              type="button"
              disabled={!isAccessible}
              onClick={() => isAccessible && onStepClick(s.step)}
              className={`group relative flex items-center space-x-3 rounded-xl p-3 text-left transition-all ${
                isActive
                  ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-900 shadow-sm'
                  : isCompleted
                  ? 'bg-zinc-50 text-zinc-900 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-850 dark:text-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 border border-zinc-200/60 dark:border-zinc-800 cursor-pointer'
                  : isAccessible
                  ? 'bg-transparent text-zinc-600 hover:bg-zinc-50 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800/60 cursor-pointer'
                  : 'cursor-not-allowed opacity-40 text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-600'
              }`}
            >
              {/* Step Icon Badge */}
              <div
                className={`relative flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg transition-transform ${
                  isActive
                    ? 'bg-zinc-800 text-white dark:bg-zinc-200 dark:text-zinc-900'
                    : isCompleted
                    ? 'bg-zinc-200 text-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200'
                    : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-400 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-500'
                }`}
              >
                {isCompleted && !isActive ? (
                  <Check className="h-4 w-4 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400" />
                ) : (
                  <Icon className="h-4 w-4" />
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Step Labels */}
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5">
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider opacity-60">
                    Step {s.step}
                  </span>
                  {s.step === 2 && sourcesCount > 0 && (
                    <span
                      className={`rounded-full px-1.5 py-0.2 text-[9px] font-mono font-bold ${
                        isActive
                          ? 'bg-zinc-800 text-white dark:bg-zinc-200 dark:text-zinc-900'
                          : 'bg-zinc-200 text-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-200'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {sourcesCount}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
                <p className="truncate text-xs font-bold mt-0.5">
                  {s.title}
                </p>
                <p className="hidden lg:block truncate text-[10px] opacity-70">
                  {s.subtitle}
                </p>
              </div>
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </nav>
  );
}

FILE: frontend/src/components/TopicInput.jsx

import React from 'react';
import {
  Tag,
  Search,
  AlertTriangle,
  GitCompare,
  CalendarClock,
  ListChecks,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react';

const CATEGORIES = [
  'Education',
  'Careers',
  'Public Services',
  'Research',
  'Business',
];

const INSPECTION_PILLARS = [
  {
    label: 'Missing Renewal Rules',
    icon: AlertTriangle,
    desc: 'Uncovers omitted eligibility clauses & hidden prerequisites',
  },
  {
    label: 'Cross-Source Conflicts',
    icon: GitCompare,
    desc: 'Highlights conflicting income limits, fees, or documents',
  },
  {
    label: 'Outdated Circulars',
    icon: CalendarClock,
    desc: 'Detects superseded circulars & stale submission methods',
  },
  {
    label: 'Verification Checklist',
    icon: ListChecks,
    desc: 'Actionable steps to resolve blind spots before committing',
  },
];

export default function TopicInput({
  topic,
  onTopicChange,
  category,
  onCategoryChange,
  onReset,
}) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-white p-5 sm:p-6 shadow-soft dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 transition-colors">
      
      {/* Header section with category dropdown */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between border-b border-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 pb-4">
        <div className="space-y-0.5">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <span className="flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-900">
              <Search className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            </span>
            <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white tracking-tight">
              1. Research Question / Decision Goal
            </h2>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
            Define what high-stakes decision, application, or policy you are investigating.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Category Picker */}
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 self-start sm:self-auto">
          <Tag className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-zinc-400 shrink-0" />
          <span className="text-xs font-medium text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">Category:</span>
          <select
            value={category}
            onChange={(e) => onCategoryChange(e.target.value)}
            className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-zinc-50 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-900 focus:border-zinc-900 focus:outline-hidden dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-100"
          >
            {CATEGORIES.map((cat) => (
              <option key={cat} value={cat}>
                {cat}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main Topic Input Field */}
      <div className="mt-4">
        <div className="relative">
          <textarea
            rows={2}
            value={topic}
            onChange={(e) => onTopicChange(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="e.g. What should I verify before applying for the State Post-Matric Scholarship? (Or describe your licensing/academic query...)"
            className="w-full resize-none rounded-xl border border-zinc-200/80 bg-zinc-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-zinc-900 placeholder-zinc-400 focus:border-zinc-900 focus:bg-white focus:outline-hidden font-medium leading-relaxed dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-850 dark:text-zinc-100 dark:placeholder-zinc-500 dark:focus:border-zinc-100 transition-all"
          />
          {topic && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onTopicChange('')}
              className="absolute top-3 right-3 text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-600 dark:hover:text-zinc-200 p-1 rounded-md"
              title="Clear topic"
            >
              <X className="h-4 w-4" />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 4 Pillars Information Banner */}
      <div className="mt-5 grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        {INSPECTION_PILLARS.map((pillar, idx) => {
          const Icon = pillar.icon;
          return (
            <div
              key={idx}
              className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-200/60 bg-zinc-50/70 p-3 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-850/50"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                <div className="flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-zinc-200 text-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200">
                  <Icon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                </div>
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white truncate">
                  {pillar.label}
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="mt-2 text-[11px] text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-500 line-clamp-2">
                {pillar.desc}
              </p>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  ShieldCheck,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronUp,
  FileCheck2,

  Info,
} from 'lucide-react';

export default function TransparencyLegendBar() {
  const [isExpanded, setIsExpanded] = useState(false);

  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-indigo-200/80 bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-50/70 via-white to-indigo-50/40 p-3.5 dark:border-indigo-900/60 dark:from-slate-900 dark:via-indigo-950/20 dark:to-slate-900 shadow-xs transition-all">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2.5">
          <span className="flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-indigo-600 text-white shadow-xs">
            <ShieldCheck className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          </span>
          <div>
            <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-950 dark:text-indigo-200">
              Epistemic Transparency & Evidence Legend
            </h4>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
              Understanding the 3 confidence and verification tiers across GapLens
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setIsExpanded(!isExpanded)}
          className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 rounded-lg px-2 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-100/60 dark:text-indigo-400 dark:hover:bg-indigo-950/60 transition-colors"
        >
          <span>{isExpanded ? 'Hide Details' : 'View Standards'}</span>
          {isExpanded ? (
            <ChevronUp className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          ) : (
            <ChevronDown className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          )}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Epistemic Tiers Grid */}
      {isExpanded && (
        <div className="mt-3.5 grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-3 border-t border-indigo-100 dark:border-slate-800 pt-3 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
          {/* Tier 1: Sourced Information */}
          <div className="rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 bg-white p-3 dark:border-emerald-900/60 dark:bg-slate-900/90 space-y-1.5 shadow-2xs">
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5">
              <span className="flex h-5 w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 dark:bg-emerald-950 dark:text-emerald-300">
                <FileCheck2 className="h-3 w-3" />
              </span>
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-300">
                Tier 1: Sourced Evidence
              </span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
              Directly extracted from user-supplied sources with traceable in-context quotes. Corroborated facts carry the highest certainty.
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Tier 2: AI-Detected Potential Signal */}
          <div className="rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-white p-3 dark:border-amber-900/60 dark:bg-slate-900/90 space-y-1.5 shadow-2xs">
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5">
              <span className="flex h-5 w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 dark:bg-amber-950 dark:text-amber-300">
                <Sparkles className="h-3 w-3" />
              </span>
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-300">
                Tier 2: AI Gap Signal
              </span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
              Inferred dark spots and missing statutory questions. Labeled as AI signals rather than verified facts to prevent false certainty.
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Tier 3: Source Contradiction & Temporal Risk */}
          <div className="rounded-xl border border-rose-200 bg-white p-3 dark:border-rose-900/60 dark:bg-slate-900/90 space-y-1.5 shadow-2xs">
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5">
              <span className="flex h-5 w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-rose-100 text-rose-700 dark:bg-rose-950 dark:text-rose-300">
                <AlertOctagon className="h-3 w-3" />
              </span>
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-rose-800 dark:text-rose-300">
                Tier 3: Conflict & Temporal Risk
              </span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
              Direct factual divergences between two sources or outdated cycle documents. Mandates verification with designated official authorities.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: frontend/src/context/ThemeContext.jsx
================================================
import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect } from 'react';

const ThemeContext = createContext();

export function ThemeProvider({ children }) {
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState(() => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      const savedTheme = localStorage.getItem('gaplens_theme');
      if (savedTheme === 'light' || savedTheme === 'dark') {
        return savedTheme;
      }
    }
    return 'light'; // Default to modern monochrome light theme
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    const root = document.documentElement;
    if (theme === 'dark') {
      root.classList.add('dark');
    } else {
      root.classList.remove('dark');
    }
    localStorage.setItem('gaplens_theme', theme);
  }, [theme]);

  const toggleTheme = () => {
    setTheme((prevTheme) => (prevTheme === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark'));
  };

  return (
    <ThemeContext.Provider value={{ theme, toggleTheme, setTheme }}>
      {children}
    </ThemeContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useTheme() {
  const context = useContext(ThemeContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useTheme must be used within a ThemeProvider');
  }
  return context;
}

================================================
FILE: frontend/src/services/api.js
